  What B+ adds to B

  B's graph paper, binder holes, handwriting, highlights and indigo desk are
  unchanged. The new layer replaces some of B's drawings with experiment
  diagrams:
  - Hero: a sample route on graph axes. The observed path is solid with
    stations, and dashed alternative branches and a faint ghost scenario fork
    from it. A yellow bead follows your pointer along the observed path and
    otherwise loops on its own.
  - Scope: the three stamped cards hang from a branching tree. Replay & Share
    sits on the solid trunk, ReShape on a dashed branch, and Advisory on a
    dotted, faint ghost branch.
  - Recorded, replayed, or made up: the table gets a trunk down its left side.
    Observed and Replayed sit on it, Synthetic branches off dashed, and Advisory
    branches again, faint.
  - Other sections: the four steps become stations on one trajectory, the three
    apps become nodes joined by edges, and Privacy shows a route with its two
    ends hatched out.

  Staying honest about what exists

  - No new words. Every diagram is wordless. I did not add "Reconstruction" or
    any other future-feature label. Your four future stages appear only through
    the site's existing words: Replay & Share, ReShape, Advisory, and the
    Observed/Replayed/Synthetic/Advisory kinds.
  - The line style encodes the site's own status stamps. Solid means built,
    dotted means replayed, dashed means planned, and faint or ghosted means
    exploratory. The tree and trunk therefore show the roadmap as a tree whose
    branches are visibly not yet real, next to the existing In progress, Planned
    and Exploratory stamps.
  - The branching diagrams carry no claims. They are decorative and aria-hidden.

  Checks

  - Copy: 932 original words, none missing. The only extra is one decorative
    aria-hidden "TripLibreLab" footer wordmark.
  - Rendering: B and B+ render at 1440, 768 and 390px with no overflow, errors
    or missing fonts. I fixed two bugs I introduced: the hero diagram overlapped
    the checklist, and the phone hero stayed two-column.
  - Not tested: the other pages from earlier concepts were re-checked only by
    their prior pass.

  Weaknesses

  The tree and trunk depict things that don't exist yet, so honesty rests on the
  stamps and the line styles. The hero is also busier than B's.

  Decision for you: B+ in place of B for TripLibreLab? If so, I'd extend it to
  the Roadmap and docs pages in that repo as a follow-up.

❯ # TripLibreLab — Final Design Selection & Implementation                      
                                                                                
  I have reviewed the latest prototype and made my final decision.              
                                                                                
  **Selected: B+ — Experimental Notebook (NEW).**                               
                                                                                
  This is the final approved design direction for TripLibreLab.                 
                                                                                
  Please proceed with implementing it in the existing TripLibreLab website.     
                                                                                
  ## 1. Authoritative Design Reference                                          
                                                                                
  Use the exact **B+ Experimental Notebook** prototype from the existing        
  scratchpad as the primary visual reference.                                   
                                                                                
  Do not use the original B, A, C, or D concepts.

  - Deep-indigo background and notebook-paper surfaces.                         
  - Graph-paper grid, binder holes, and notebook margin lines.

  - Travel trajectories with nodes, branches, and alternative paths.            
  - The balance between an approachable notebook and a computational experiment 
  workbench.                                                                    
                                                                                
  **Do not simplify the selected design into a generic SaaS website, dashboard, 
  or conventional card layout.**

  Authorized repository:

  `./TripLibreLab/docs/`

  components to faithfully implement B+.

  configuration.

  - `./TripLibre/`                                                              
  - `./hhtdiary.github.io/`                                                     
                                                                                
  Preserve all existing local changes in those repositories.                    
                                                                                
  ## 3. Content Preservation                                                    
                                                                                
  This remains a visual redesign, not a content rewrite.                        
                                                                                
  You MUST preserve:                                                            
                                                                                
  - Every existing published heading, paragraph, label, description, and        
  acknowledgment.                                                               
  - All existing screenshots, logos, SVGs, illustrations, and images.           
  - Original image files and their content.                                     
  - All existing links, navigation destinations, and page availability.         
  - Existing functionality and interactive behavior.                            
  - The original logical reading order.                                         
                                                                                
  Do not delete, rewrite, shorten, or invent content.                           
                                                                                
  Do not introduce fictional product capabilities, statistics, testimonials, or 
  screenshots.                                                                  
                                                                                
  ### Future Experimental Features                                              
                                                                                
  The design should accommodate future Replay, Reconstruction, Simulation, and  
  Advisory functionality.                                                       
                                                                                
  However, do not present unfinished features as currently available.           
                                                                                
  Use the visual language of experimentation without adding unsupported product 
  claims or nonfunctional feature controls.                                     
                                                                                
  ## 4. Implementation Requirements                                             
                                                                                
  Use the approved B+ prototype as the authoritative reference.                 
                                                                                
  Extend its design language throughout the existing TripLibreLab website,      
  adapting compositions appropriately for different page types.                 
                                                                                
  Preserve the following qualities:                                             
                                                                                
  - Playfulness and visual personality.                                         
  - Strong notebook identity.                                                   
  - Clear experimental and computational character.                             
  - Legible body typography.                                                    
  - Thoughtful handwritten accents.                                             
  - Distinctive but purposeful visual interactions.                             
  - Responsive desktop and mobile layouts.                                      
                                                                                
  Avoid excessive decoration that interferes with reading or usability.         
                                                                                
  Keep the implementation lightweight and maintainable.                         
                                                                                
  Do not add unnecessary JavaScript libraries or large dependencies solely for  
  visual effects.                                                               
                                                                                
  ## 5. Accessibility and Motion                                                
                                                                                
  Ensure:                                                                       
                                                                                
  - Accessible contrast ratios.                                                 
  - Keyboard-accessible navigation and controls.                                
  - Visible focus indicators.                                                   
  - Respect for `prefers-reduced-motion`.                                       
  - No content hidden or clipped on mobile.                                     
  - No page-level horizontal overflow.                                          
  - All existing links and interactions remain functional.                      
                                                                                
  Lightweight experimental animations are welcome when they improve the         
  experience, but they must not distract from the content.                      
                                                                                
  ## 6. Verification                                                            
                                                                                
  After implementation:                                                         
                                                                                
  1. Compare the implemented website against the B+ prototype.                  
  2. Capture desktop screenshots at 1440px.                                     
  3. Capture tablet screenshots at 768px.                                       
  4. Capture mobile screenshots at 390px.                                       
  5. Verify all published text against the original version.                    
  6. Compare image asset SHA-256 hashes.                                        
  7. Test navigation and interactive elements.                                  
  8. Check accessibility and browser console errors.                            
  9. Confirm no files outside the authorized website scope were modified.       
  10. Provide a concise Git diff summary.                                       
                                                                                
  Preserve the current B+ prototype and its screenshots for reference.          
                                                                                
  ## 7. Final Deliverables                                                      
                                                                                
  Provide:                                                                      
                                                                                
  - Implementation summary.
